(140)

D.3.2
Computing
gE(U)
.

According to Equation (
86
) and Equation (
184
), we have:

\displaystyle gE(U)

\displaystyle=\left(\left([gE(W)]^{(Q,i)},[gE(W)]^{(K,i)},[gE(W)]^{(V,i)},[gE(W)]^{(O,i)}\right)_{i=1,\ldots,h},\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.\left([gE(W)]^{(A)},[gE(W)]^{(B)}\right),\left([gE(b)]^{(A)},[gE(b)]^{(B)}\right)\right),

where

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\left[[E(W)]^{(Q,\tau(i))}\cdot\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{\top}\right]_{j,k},

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(K,i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\left[[E(W)]^{(K,\tau(i))}\cdot\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\right]_{j,k},

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(V,i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\left[[E(W)]^{(V,\tau(i))}\cdot N^{(\tau(i))}\right]_{j,k},

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\left[\left(N^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\cdot[E(W)]^{(O,\tau(i))}\right]_{j,\pi_{O}(k)},

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=[E(W)]^{(A)}_{\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)},

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(B)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=[E(W)]^{(B)}_{\pi_{A}(j),k},

\displaystyle[gE(b)]^{(A)}_{k}

\displaystyle=[E(b)]^{(A)}_{\pi_{A}(k)},

\displaystyle[gE(b)]^{(B)}_{k}

\displaystyle=[E(b)]^{(B)}_{k}.

We calculate these entries in detail below.

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k}=\left[[E(W)]^{(Q,\tau(i))}\cdot\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{\top}\right]_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}[E(W)]^{(Q,\tau(i))}_{j,l}\cdot\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{\top}_{l,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}M^{(\tau(i))}_{k,l}\cdot\left\{{\begin{aligned} &\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(Q,s):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(K,s):p,q}[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(V,s):p,q}[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(O,s):p,q}[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}\end{aligned}}\right\}.

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(K,i)}_{j,k}=\left[[E(W)]^{(K,\tau(i))}\cdot\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\right]_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}[E(W)]^{(K,\tau(i))}_{j,l}\cdot\left(\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\right)_{l,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}[E(W)]^{(K,\tau(i))}_{j,l}\cdot\left(\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\right)_{l,k}

(141)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}\left(\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\right)_{l,k}\cdot\left\{{\begin{aligned} &\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(Q,s):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(K,s):p,q}[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(V,s):p,q}[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(O,s):p,q}[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,l}\end{aligned}}\right\}.

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(V,i)}_{j,k}=\left[[E(W)]^{(V,\tau(i))}\cdot N^{(\tau(i))}\right]_{j,k},

(142)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}[E(W)]^{(V,\tau(i))}_{j,l}\cdot N^{(\tau(i))}_{l,k}

(143)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}N^{(\tau(i))}_{l,k}\cdot\left\{{\begin{aligned} &\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(Q,s):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(K,s):p,q}[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(V,s):p,q}[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(O,s):p,q}[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,l}\end{aligned}}\right\}.

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}=\left[\left(N^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\cdot[E(W)]^{(O,\tau(i))}\right]_{j,\pi_{O}(k)},

(144)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}\left(\left(N^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\right)_{j,l}\cdot[E(W)]^{(O,\tau(i))}_{l,\pi_{O}(k)}

(145)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}\left(\left(N^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\right)_{j,l}\cdot\left\{{\begin{aligned} &\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(Q,s):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(K,s):p,q}[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(V,s):p,q}[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(O,s):p,q}[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):l,\pi_{O}(k)}\end{aligned}}\right\}.

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=[E(W)]^{(A)}_{\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)},

(146)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(Q,s):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(K,s):p,q}[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(V,s):p,q}[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(O,s):p,q}[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}.

(147)

\displaystyle[gE(W)]^{(B)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=[E(W)]^{(B)}_{\pi_{A}(j),k},

(148)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(Q,s):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(K,s):p,q}[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(V,s):p,q}[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(O,s):p,q}[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}

(149)

\displaystyle[gE(b)]^{(A)}_{k}

\displaystyle=[E(b)]^{(A)}_{\pi_{A}(k)},

(150)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(Q,s):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(K,s):p,q}[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(V,s):p,q}[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(O,s):p,q}[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}.

(151)

\displaystyle[gE(b)]^{(B)}_{k}

\displaystyle=[E(b)]^{(B)}_{k}

(152)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(Q,s):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(K,s):p,q}[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(V,s):p,q}[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(O,s):p,q}[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(B):k}.

(153)

D.3.3
Coefficients comparison in the equation
E(gU)=gE(U)

Since
E(gU)=gE(U)
, we must have:

\displaystyle[E(gW)]^{(Q,i)}

\displaystyle=[gE(W)]^{(Q,i)},

\displaystyle[E(gW)]^{(K,i)}

\displaystyle=[gE(W)]^{(K,i)},

\displaystyle[E(gW)]^{(V,i)}

\displaystyle=[gE(W)]^{(V,i)},

\displaystyle[E(gW)]^{(O,i)}

\displaystyle=[gE(W)]^{(O,i)},

\displaystyle[E(gW)]^{(A)}

\displaystyle=[gE(W)]^{(A)},

\displaystyle[E(gW)]^{(B)}

\displaystyle=[gE(W)]^{(B)},

\displaystyle[E(gb)]^{(A)}

\displaystyle=[gE(b)]^{(A)},

\displaystyle[E(gb)]^{(B)}

\displaystyle=[gE(b)]^{(B)},

and these equalities hold for all
i=1,\ldots,h
,
U\in\mathcal{U}
and
g\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
.

In the following, we will solve these equalities, one by one, to find the constraints of the parameters
\Phi^{-}_{-}
s of
E(U)
.

Case 1. Solve the equation
[E(gW)]^{(Q,i)}=[gE(W)]^{(Q,i)}
.

For every
j,k
, we have

[E(gW)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k}=[gE(W)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k},

or equivalently,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(QK,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(VO,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(Q,s)}}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{\top}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(K,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{(K,s)}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(V,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(V,s)}}\cdot N^{(s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(O,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}\left[\left(N^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\cdot[W]^{(O,s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{O}^{-1}(p),\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(B):\pi_{A}^{-1}(p),q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}[E(W)]^{(Q,\tau(i))}_{j,l}\cdot\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{\top}_{l,k}.

(154)

Observe that the right hand side of the above equation is just a linear function on
M^{(\tau(i))}
. By applying Corollary
D.3
, we see that all terms of the left hand side are equal to zero, except those containing
M^{(\tau(i))}
will be identically equal to the right hand side, i.e.

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(QK,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(VO,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}s=1\\
s\neq\tau(i)\end{subarray}}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(Q,s)}}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{\top}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(K,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{(K,s)}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(V,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(V,s)}}\cdot N^{(s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(O,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}\left[\left(N^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\cdot[W]^{(O,s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{O}^{-1}(p),\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(B):\pi_{A}^{-1}(p),q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad=0,

(155)

and

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(Q,\tau(i))}}\cdot\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{\top}\right]_{p,q}=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}[E(W)]^{(Q,\tau(i))}_{j,l}\cdot\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{\top}_{l,k}.

(156)

We choose
g
to be the identity of
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
(i.e.
\tau
,
\pi_{O}
,
\pi_{A}
,
M^{(s)}
and
N^{(s)}
are all identities) in Equation (
155
), then substituting the obtained result into Equation (
102
), we obtain a more compact formula for
[E(W)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k}
as follows:

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k}=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,i)}}_{p,q}.

(157)

Then by substituting this compact formula of
[E(W)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k}
to Equation (
156
), we obtain the first constraints:

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(Q,\tau(i))}}\cdot\left(M^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{\top}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}\left(\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,l}_{(Q,\tau(i)):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,\tau(i))}}_{p,q}\right)\cdot M^{(\tau(i))}_{k,l},

or equivalently,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,k}_{(Q,\tau(i)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(Q,i)}}\cdot\left(M^{(i)}\right)^{\top}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}\left(\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,l}_{(Q,i):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,i)}}_{p,q}\right)\cdot M^{(i)}_{k,l},

(158)

We view the left hand side of Equation (
158
) as a sum of linear functions on the
q
-th rows of
M^{(i)}
with
q=1,\ldots,D_{k}
, while the right hand side is just a linear function on the
k
-th row of
M^{(i)}
.
The by applying Corollary
D.3
, we see that all of the coefficients
\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,k}_{(Q,\tau(i)):p,q}
of the left side are equal to zero, except those with the indices
k=q
.
Therefore, we can reduce the formula for
[E(W)]^{(Q,i)}
and the constraint further as follows:

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k}=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,k}[W]^{{(Q,i)}}_{p,k}.

(159)

To find the constraints for the coefficients, we observe that, the left hand side of Equation (
158
) depends on
\tau
, while the right hand side is not.
Therefore, by varying
\tau
, the left hand side does not change.
As a consequence, we must have

\displaystyle\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,k}_{(Q,\tau(i)):p,k}=\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,k},

(160)

for all
i,j,p,k
and
\tau
.

Next, by substituting Equation (
159
) and Equation (
160
) to Equation (
156
), we obtain:

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,k}\left(\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}[W]^{{(Q,i)}}_{p,l}\cdot M^{(i)}_{k,l}\right)=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{k}}\left(\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,l}_{(Q,i):p,l}[W]^{{(Q,i)}}_{p,l}\right)\cdot M^{(i)}_{k,l}.

(161)

By comparing the corresponding coefficients both sides, we finally obtain the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,k}_{(Q,\tau(i)):p,k}=\Phi^{(Q,i):j,l}_{(Q,i):p,l},

(162)

for all
i,j,p,k,l
and
\tau
.

One can verify directly that
[E(W)]^{(K,i)}_{j,k}
defined in the form

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k}=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,k}[W]^{{(Q,i)}}_{p,k},

(163)

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,k}=\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,k^{\prime}}_{(Q,\tau(i)):p,k^{\prime}},

(164)

for all
i,j,p,k,k^{\prime},\tau
, satisfies the constraint in Equation (
156
).

Case 2. Solve the equation
[E(gW)]^{(K,i)}=[gE(W)]^{(K,i)}
.

By using the same argument, we also obtain
[E(W)]^{(K,i)}_{j,k}
in the form

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(K,i)}_{j,k}=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\Phi^{(K,i):j,k}_{(K,i):p,k}[W]^{(K,i)}_{p,k},

(165)

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(K,i):j,k}_{(K,i):p,k}=\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,k^{\prime}}_{(K,\tau(i)):p,k^{\prime}},

(166)

for all
i,j,p,k,k^{\prime},\tau
.

Case 3. Solve the equation
[E(gW)]^{(V,i)}=[gE(W)]^{(V,i)}
.

By using the same argument, we also obtain
[E(W)]^{(V,i)}_{j,k}
in the form

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(V,i)}_{j,k}=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\Phi^{(V,i):j,k}_{(V,i):p,k}[W]^{(V,i)}_{p,k},

(167)

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(V,i):j,k}_{(V,i):p,k}=\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,k^{\prime}}_{(V,\tau(i)):p,k^{\prime}},

(168)

for all
i,j,p,k,k^{\prime},\tau
.

Case 4. Solve the equation
[E(gW)]^{(O,i)}=[gE(W)]^{(O,i)}
.

Since

[E(gW)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}=[gE(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k},

it follows from Equation (
105
) that

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(QK,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(VO,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(Q,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(Q,s)}}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{\top}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(K,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{(K,s)}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(V,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(V,s)}}\cdot N^{(s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}\left[\left(N^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\cdot[W]^{(O,s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{O}^{-1}(p),\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(B):\pi_{A}^{-1}(p),q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}\left(\left(N^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\right)_{j,l}\cdot[E(W)]^{(O,\tau(i))}_{l,\pi_{O}(k)}.

(169)

The right hand side of the above equation is just a linear function on
\left(N^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}
.
Therefore, it follows from Corollary
D.3
that all terms of the left hand side are equal to zero, except those containing
\left(N^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}
will be identically equal to the right hand side, i.e.

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(QK,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(VO,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(Q,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(Q,s)}}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{\top}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(K,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{(K,s)}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(V,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(V,s)}}\cdot N^{(s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}s=1\\
s\neq\tau(i)\end{subarray}}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}\left[\left(N^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\cdot[W]^{(O,s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{O}^{-1}(p),\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(B):\pi_{A}^{-1}(p),q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad=0.

(170)

By choosing
g
to be the identity element of
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
in Equation (
170
), and substituting the obtained result to Equation (
184
), we obtain a shorter formula for
[E(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}
as follow:

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,i):p,q}[W]^{(O,i)}_{p,q}.

(171)

Thus, the constraint becomes

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,i):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}\left[\left(N^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\cdot[W]^{(O,\tau(i))}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}\left(\left(N^{(\tau(i))}\right)^{-1}\right)_{j,l}\cdot[E(W)]^{(O,\tau(i))}_{l,\pi_{O}(k)},

or equivalently,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):j,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(O,\tau(i)):p,\pi_{O}(q)}\left[\left(N^{(i)}\right)^{-1}\cdot[W]^{(O,i)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}\left(\left(N^{(i)}\right)^{-1}\right)_{j,l}\cdot[E(W)]^{(O,i)}_{l,k}.

(172)

Observe that, the left hand side of Equation (
172
) is just a linear function on the
j
-th row of
\left(N^{(i)}\right)^{-1}
, while the left hand side is a sum of linear combination of
p
-th rows of
\left(N^{(i)}\right)^{-1}
with
p=1,\ldots,D_{v}
.
Therefore, only coefficients of
[E(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}
with
p=j
are nonzero, i.e

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}=\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,i):j,q}[W]^{(O,i)}_{j,q}.

(173)

In addition, since the right hand side of Equation (
172
) is independent of
\tau
and
\pi_{O}
, the left hand side must remain unchange if we vary
\tau
and
\pi_{O}
.
As a consequence, we have

\displaystyle\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):j,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(O,\tau(i)):j,\pi_{O}(q)}=\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,i):j,q}

(174)

for all
i,j,k,q,\tau
and
\pi_{O}
.
Next, by substituting Equation (
173
) and Equation (
174
) to Equation (
172
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):j,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(O,\tau(i)):j,\pi_{O}(q)}\left[\left(N^{(i)}\right)^{-1}\cdot[W]^{(O,i)}\right]_{j,q}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}\left(\left(N^{(i)}\right)^{-1}\right)_{j,l}\cdot\left(\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):l,k}_{(O,i):l,q}[W]^{(O,i)}_{l,q}\right),

or equivalently,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):j,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(O,\tau(i)):j,\pi_{O}(q)}\left(\left(N^{(i)}\right)^{-1}\right)_{j,l}\cdot[W]^{(O,i)}_{l,q}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(O,i):l,k}_{(O,i):l,q}\left(\left(N^{(i)}\right)^{-1}\right)_{j,l}\cdot[W]^{(O,i)}_{l,q}.

(175)

By comparing corresponding coefficients both sides, we have

\displaystyle\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):j,\pi_{O}(k)}_{(O,\tau(i)):j,\pi_{O}(q)}=\Phi^{(O,i):l,k}_{(O,i):l,q}.

(176)

Finally, one can verify directly that
[E(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}
defined as

[E(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}
with
p=j
are nonzero, i.e

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}=\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,i):j,q}[W]^{(O,i)}_{j,q},

(177)

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,i):j,q}=\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):j^{\prime},\pi_{O}(k)}_{(O,\tau(i)):j^{\prime},\pi_{O}(q)}

(178)

satisfy the constraint in Equation (
172
) for all
i,j,j^{\prime},k,q
and
\tau,\pi_{O}
.

Case 5. Solve the equation
[E(gW)]^{(A)}=[gE(W)]^{(A)}
.

Since
[E(gW)]^{(A)}_{j,k}=[gE(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(QK,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(VO,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(Q,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(Q,s)}}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{\top}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(K,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{(K,s)}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(V,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(V,s)}}\cdot N^{(s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(O,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}\left[\left(N^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\cdot[W]^{(O,s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{O}^{-1}(p),\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):\pi_{A}^{-1}(p),q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(A):j,k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(Q,s):p,q}[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(K,s):p,q}[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(V,s):p,q}[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(O,s):p,q}[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}.

(179)

We observe that the left hand side is a sum of linear functions on the matrices
M^{(s)}
,
\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{-1}
,
N^{(s)}
, and
\left(N^{(s)}\right)^{-1}
, while the right hand side is independent of these matrices.
Therefore, according to Corollary
D.3
, the terms containing these matrices in the left hand side must be equal to zero.
For the remaining terms, we can use Lemma
D.1
to compare the corresponding ones with those in the right hand side and obtain the constraints:

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(QK,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(QK,s):p,q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(VO,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(VO,s):p,q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(Q,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(K,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(V,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(O,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{O}^{-1}(p),\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):p,q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):\pi_{A}^{-1}(p),q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):p,q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(Q,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(K,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(V,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(O,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}.

One can verify directly that
[E(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}
defined by

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(A):j,k},

(180)

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}.

for all
j,j^{\prime},k,k^{\prime},s,s^{\prime},p,p^{\prime},q,q^{\prime},\pi_{O},\pi_{A}
satisfies the condition
[E(gW)]^{(A)}_{j,k}=[gE(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}
.

Case 6: Solve the equation
[E(gW)]^{(B)}=[gE(W)]^{(B)}
.

By using the similar argument as Case 5, we also see that:

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(B)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(B):j,k},

(181)

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}.

for all
j,k,s,p,q,\tau,\pi_{O},\pi_{A}
,
satisfies the constraint
[E(gW)]^{(B)}_{j,k}=[gE(W)]^{(B)}_{j,k}
.

Case 7. Solve the equation
[E(gb)]^{(A)}=[gE(b)]^{(A)}
.

By using the same argument as Case 5, we also see that
[E(b)]^{(A)}_{k}
defined by

\displaystyle[E(b)]^{(A)}_{k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\phi^{(A):k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(A):k},

(182)

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}.

for all
j,k,s,p,q,\tau,\pi_{O},\pi_{A}
,
satisfies the constraint
[E(gW)]^{(A)}_{j,k}=[gE(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}
.

Case 8. Solve the equation
[E(gb)]^{(B)}=[gE(b)]^{(B)}
.

By using the same argument as in Case 5, we see that
[E(b)]^{(B)}_{q}
defined by

\displaystyle[E(b)]^{(B)}_{k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(B):k},

(183)

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k},

for all
j,k,s,p,q,\tau,\pi_{A},\pi_{O}
,
satisfies the constraint
[E(gW)]^{(A)}_{j,k}=[gE(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}
.

D.4
Final formula for the equivariant polynomial layer

We gather the results of the computations of the equivariant layer in the previous section here for further implementation.

Theorem D.4
.

The map
E\colon\mathcal{U}\rightarrow\mathcal{U}
with

\displaystyle E(U)

\displaystyle=\biggl(\left([E(W)]^{(Q,i)},[E(W)]^{(K,i)},[E(W)]^{(V,i)},[E(W)]^{(O,i)}\right)_{i=1,\ldots,h},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left([E(W)]^{(A)},[E(W)]^{(B)}\right),\left([E(b)]^{(A)},[E(b)]^{(B)}\right)\biggr),

(184)

is
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
-equivariant.

Here, the components of
E(U)
are given below.

1.

[E(W)]^{(Q,i)}
is defined as

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(Q,i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,k}[W]^{{(Q,i)}}_{p,k},

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(Q,i):j,k}_{(Q,i):p,k}=\Phi^{(Q,\tau(i)):j,k^{\prime}}_{(Q,\tau(i)):p,k^{\prime}},

2.

[E(W)]^{(K,i)}
is defined as

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(K,i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\Phi^{(K,i):j,k}_{(K,i):p,k}[W]^{(K,i)}_{p,k},

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(K,i):j,k}_{(K,i):p,k}=\Phi^{(K,\tau(i)):j,k^{\prime}}_{(K,\tau(i)):p,k^{\prime}},

3.

[E(W)]^{(V,i)}
is defined by

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(V,i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\Phi^{(V,i):j,k}_{(V,i):p,k}[W]^{(V,i)}_{p,k},

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(V,i):j,k}_{(V,i):p,k}=\Phi^{(V,\tau(i)):j,k^{\prime}}_{(V,\tau(i)):p,k^{\prime}},

4.

[E(W)]^{(O,i)}
is defined by

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(O,i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,i):j,q}[W]^{(O,i)}_{j,q},

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(O,i):j,k}_{(O,i):j,q}=\Phi^{(O,\tau(i)):j^{\prime},\pi_{O}(k)}_{(O,\tau(i)):j^{\prime},\pi_{O}(q)}\par

5.

[E(W)]^{(A)}
is defined by

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(A):j,k},

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):q},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{O}(j),\pi_{A}(k)}.

6.

[E(W)]^{(B)}
is defined by

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(B)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(B):j,k},

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):\pi_{A}(j),k}.

7.

[E(b)]^{(A)}
is defined by

\displaystyle[E(b)]^{(A)}_{k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\phi^{(A):k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(A):k},

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(A):k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(A):\pi_{A}(k)}.

8.

[E(b)]^{(B)}
is defined by

\displaystyle[E(b)]^{(B)}_{k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(QK,s):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(VO,s):p,q}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):p,q}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):p,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):q}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(B):k},

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):q}

\displaystyle\Phi^{(B):k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(B):k},

for all
j,j^{\prime},k,k^{\prime},s,s^{\prime},p,p^{\prime},q,q^{\prime}
and
\tau,\pi_{O},\pi_{A}
.

Appendix E
An Invariant Polynomial Layer

In this section, we will construct a map
I\colon\mathcal{U}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{D^{\prime}}
such that
I
is equivariant with respect to
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
, i.e.

\displaystyle I(gU)=I(U),

(185)

for all
U\in\mathcal{U}
and
g\in\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
.

E.1
A general form with unknown coefficients

We select
I(U)
to be a linear combination of entries of the matrices
[W]^{(Q,s)}
,
[W]^{(K,s)}
,
[W]^{(V,s)}
,
[W]^{(O,s)}
,
[W]^{(A)}
,
[W]^{(B)}
,
[b]^{(A)}
,
[b]^{(B)}
as well as the matrices
[WW]^{(QK,s)}
and
[WW]^{(VO,s)}
, i.e

\displaystyle I(U)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(QK,s):p,q}\cdot[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(VO,s):p,q}\cdot[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi_{(Q,s):p,q}\cdot[W]^{{(Q,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi_{(K,s):p,q}\cdot[W]^{(K,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi_{(V,s):p,q}\cdot[W]^{{(V,s)}}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(O,s):p,q}\cdot[W]^{(O,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi_{(A):p,q}\cdot[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(B):p,q}\cdot[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi_{(A):q}\cdot[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(B):q}\cdot[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi_{1},

(186)

where the coefficients
\Phi_{-}
s are matrices of size
D^{\prime}\times 1
.

In the following, we will determine constraints on the coefficients such that
I
will be
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
-invariant under these constraints.

E.2
Compute
I(gU)

By using the same arguments in the computation of
E(gU)
, we have

\displaystyle I(gW)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(QK,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(VO,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi_{(Q,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(Q,s)}}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{\top}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{k}}\Phi_{(K,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{(K,s)}\cdot\left(M^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{v}}\Phi_{(V,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}\left[[W]^{{(V,s)}}\cdot N^{(s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{v}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(O,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}\left[\left(N^{(s)}\right)^{-1}\cdot[W]^{(O,s)}\right]_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi_{(A):\pi_{O}^{-1}(p),\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(B):\pi_{A}^{-1}(p),q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi_{(A):\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi.

(187)

E.3
Compare
I(gU)
and
I(U)

According to Lemma
D.1
, in order to have
I(gU)=I(U)
, the corresponding coefficients of
I(gU)
and
I(U)
must be equal.
As a consequence, we have

\displaystyle\Phi_{(QK,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(VO,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(Q,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Phi_{(K,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Phi_{(V,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Phi_{(O,\tau^{-1}(s)):p,\pi_{O}^{-1}(q)}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Phi_{(A):\pi_{O}^{-1}(p),\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(B):\pi_{A}^{-1}(p),q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(A):\pi_{A}^{-1}(q)}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(A):q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(B):q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{1}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{1},

or equivalently,

\displaystyle\Phi_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(Q,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Phi_{(K,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Phi_{(V,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Phi_{(O,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Phi_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(B):q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{1}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{1}.

E.4
Final formula for the invariant polynomial layer

We summarize the above computation here for implementation.

Theorem E.1
.

The map
I\colon\mathcal{U}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{D^{\prime}}
defined by

\displaystyle I(U)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(QK,s):p,q}\cdot[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(VO,s):p,q}\cdot[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi_{(A):p,q}\cdot[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(B):p,q}\cdot[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi_{(A):q}\cdot[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(B):q}\cdot[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi_{1},

(188)

with the constraints

\displaystyle\Phi_{(QK,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(QK,\tau(s)):p,q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(VO,s):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(VO,\tau(s)):p,\pi_{O}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(A):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(A):\pi_{O}(p),\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(B):p,q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(B):\pi_{A}(p),q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(A):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(A):\pi_{A}(q)}

\displaystyle\Phi_{(B):q}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{(B):q}

\displaystyle\Phi_{1}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{1}.

is
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
-equivariant.

Appendix F
Computation Complexity of Equivariant and Invariant Layers

The computational complexity of the Transformer-NFN is derived from its invariant and equivariant layers as outlined in their pseudocode (Appendix
I.2
,
I.3
):

•

Equivariant Layer Complexity:

\mathcal{O}(d\cdot e\cdot h\cdot D^{2}\cdot\max(D_{q},D_{k},D_{v}))

•

Invariant Layer Complexity:

\mathcal{O}(d\cdot e\cdot D^{\prime}\cdot D\cdot\max(D\cdot h,D_{A}))

Where the parameters follow our notation in Table
8
.

The Transformer-NFN implementation leverages optimized tensor contraction operations (e.g.,
einsum
), enabling efficient and highly parallelizable computations on modern GPUs. This ensures computational practicality while delivering significant performance improvements.

Appendix G
Additional Dataset Details

Figure 1:
Accuracy histogram of MNIST task and AGNews task in the Small Transformer Zoo. The number of samples is showed in log scale for improved visibility.

To create a wide range of transformer model, we opt to vary six hyperparameters in our experiments: train fraction, optimizer (SGD, SGDm, Adam, or RMSprop), learning rate, L2 regularization coefficient, weight initialization standard deviation, and dropout probability. The train fraction determines the proportion of the original training dataset used, while the optimizer dictates the algorithm for parameter updates. Learning rate, L2 regularization, and weight initialization standard deviation control various aspects of the training process, and dropout probability helps in preventing overfitting.

We select a range of typical values for each hyperparameter independently, then generate all possible combinations to form our set of hyperparameter configurations. During our preliminary experiments, we observed that the optimal range of hyperparameters varies significantly between optimizer types. Consequently, we divided our settings into two categories: Adam-RMSprop and SGD-SGDm. Table
4
provides a detailed overview of our hyperparameter configurations. Overall, there are
8000
configurations for each category, resulting in
16000
configurations in total. These configurations are consistently applied across both tasks to ensure comparability. All models are trained for
100
epochs, with checkpoints and accuracy measurements recorded at epochs
50
,
75
,
100
, and at the epoch with the best accuracy. During the process, we eliminate any runs that crash.

MNIST-Transformers.

The MNIST dataset
(
LeCun & Cortes, 2005
)
is a widely-used benchmark in computer vision, consisting of
28\times 28
pixel grayscale images of handwritten digits (
0
-
9
). For this vision task, the model objective is to perform digit classification. The embedding component first applies a 2D convolution to encode image patches and then adds a fixed positional encoding to provide spatial information. The encoder, comprising two stacked transformer blocks, processes these embeddings to capture complex relationships between different parts of the image. The classifier applies global average pooling to the encoder’s output and then passes it through two fully connected layers with a ReLU activation in between, finally outputting probabilities for ten classes corresponding to the digits
0
-
9
. Using our hyperparameter configurations, we generate a total of
62756
model samples for the MNIST task, including
15689
checkpoints at epochs
50
,
75
,
100
, and the best-performing epoch, each trained with a distinct combination of hyperparameters. The accuracy distribution for MNIST in Figure
1
displays a strong concentration in the
80\%
to
100\%
range while remaining models distribute almost uniformly across lower accuracies, with a slight increase around
10\%
.

AGNews-Transformers.

The AG’s News Topic Classification Dataset
(
Zhang et al., 2015
)
is a collection of news articles from the AG’s corpus of news articles on the web, categorized into four classes: World, Sports, Business, and Sci/Tech. We take the description of the articles input and train models to predict its corresponding topic. Our transformer-based model is adapted for this task as follows: The embedding component uses a pre-trained Word2Vec model to map each token to an embedding vector. These embeddings are combined with fixed positional encodings to retain sequential information. The encoder, consisting of two stacked transformer blocks, processes these embeddings to capture contextual relationships within the text. The classifier applies global average pooling to the encoder’s output, then passes it through two fully connected layers with a ReLU activation in between, finally outputting probabilities for the four categories. Our experiments on this task yield a diverse set of
63796
model checkpoints, derived from
15949
unique model configurations. These checkpoints are collected at four key points during training: epochs
50
,
75
,
100
, and at the epoch of peak performance. The accuracy distribution for AGNews in Figure
1
shows a notable concentration in the
50\%
to
90\%
range, with a peak around
80\%
while also exhibiting a smaller cluster of models around
25\%
.

H.1
General details

Training details
The models were trained for a total of 50 epochs, using a batch size of 16. We employed the Adam optimizer with a maximum learning rate of
{10}^{-3}
. A linear warmup strategy was applied to the learning rate, spanning the initial 10 epochs for gradual warmup. We utilize Binary Cross Entropy for the loss function.

Number of parameters

Table
6
summarizes the total parameter count for all models. The architectural details and hyperparameter configurations for each model are provided in
H.2
and
H.3
. Importantly, we optimized the baseline models to their best configurations, and further increasing the number of parameters would likely result in overfitting.

H.2
Archiecture and hyperparameters of Transformer-NFN

The Transformer-NFN model is composed of three main components designed to handle the input weights of a transformer network. For the embedding and classifier components, both of which are MLP-based, we employ a standard MLP with ReLU activation to process individual component. The transformer block itself is handled by an invariant architecture, incorporating several equivariant polynomial layers of Transformer-NFN. These layers utilize ReLU activation, which specifically operates on the two MLP components within the transformer block. Following this, the output is passed through an invariant polynomial layer of Transformer-NFN. The outputs of each of these components are represented as vectors, which are concatenated and passed through a final MLP head with Sigmoid activation for prediction.

In our experimental setup, the embedding component is modeled using a single-layer MLP with 10 hidden neurons, while the classifier component is a two-layer MLP, each layer containing 10 hidden neurons. For the invariant architecture of Transformer-NFN, we apply an equivariant polynomial layer of Transformer-NFN with 10 hidden channels to process the weights, followed by an invariant polynomial layer of Transformer-NFN and an MLP that outputs a 10-dimensional vector. The resulting vectors from these components are concatenated and passed through a single classification head to generate the final prediction.

H.3
Architecture and hyperparameters for other baselines

Here we describe the architecture of all baselines:

•

MLP
In the MLP model, the weights of all components are first flattened and processed through a separate MLP for each component. Specifically, the MLP handling the transformer block and embedding components consists of a single layer with 50 hidden neurons, while the classifier component is modeled using a two-layer MLP, each layer containing 50 neurons. The outputs from all components are concatenated and passed through a final MLP to produce the prediction.

•

STATNN

(
Unterthiner et al., 2020
)
For the STATNN model, we adapt the original approach to work with the transformer block. In this case, we compute statistical features from the weights of the query, key, value, output, as well as the weights and biases of the two linear layers. These features are concatenated and passed through a single-layer MLP with 256 hidden neurons. The classifier component uses the original STATNN model to extract features, which are then processed through an MLP with 256 hidden neurons. For the embedding component, a single-layer MLP with 64 hidden neurons is employed. The outputs from all components are concatenated and passed through a single-layer MLP for the final prediction.

•

XGBoost

(
Chen & Guestrin, 2016
)
,
LightGBM

(
Ke et al., 2017
)
,
Random Forest

(
Breiman, 2001
)
: We flattened the weights of all components and directly employed popular gradient boosting libraries for regression. The hyperparameters for all three tree-based models were set to a maximum depth of 10, a minimum child weight of 50, and a maximum of 256 leaves.

Figure 2:
Visualization of all models on test set of AGNews-Transformers dataset.

H.4
Experiment on augmented AGNews-Transformers dataset

Experiment Setup

In this experiment, we evaluate the performance of Transformer-NFN on the AGNews-Transformers dataset augmented using the group action
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
. We perform a 2-fold augmentation for both the train and test sets. The original weights are retained, and additional weights are constructed by applying permutations and scaling transformations to transformer modules. The elements in
M
and
N
(see Section
4.3
) are uniformly sampled across
[-1,1]
,
[-10,10]
, and
[-100,100]
.

Results

The results for all models are presented in Table
7
. The findings demonstrate that Transformer-NFN maintains stable Kendall’s
\tau
across different ranges of scale operators. Notably, as the weights are augmented, the performance of Transformer-NFN improves, whereas the performance of other baseline methods declines significantly. This performance disparity results in a widening gap between Transformer-NFN and the second-best model, increasing from 0.031 to 0.082.

The general decline observed in baseline models highlights their lack of symmetry. In contrast, Transformer-NFN’s equivariance to
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
ensures stability and even slight performance improvements under augmentation. This underscores the importance of defining the maximal symmetric group, to which Transformer-NFN is equivariant, in overcoming the limitations of baseline methods.

Appendix I
Implementation of Equivariant and Invariant Layers

We present the multi-channel implementations of the
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
-equivariant map
E\colon\mathcal{U}^{d}\rightarrow\mathcal{U}^{e}
and the
\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{U}}
-invariant map
I\colon\mathcal{U}^{d}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{e\times D^{\prime}}
. We summarize the Equivariant and Invariant Layers with the bullet notation
\bullet
and adopt einops-like pseudocode to maintain consistency and standardization in transformer weight space manipulations.

To facilitate understanding of the implementation, we summarize the key dimensions involved in Table
8
and define the shapes of the input terms in Table
9
.

Table 8:
Summary of key dimensions involved in the implementation

Symbol

Description

d

Number of input channels for the equivariant and invariant layer

e

Number of output channels for the equivariant and invariant layer

D

Embedding dimension of the input and output sequences of the transformer block

D_{k}=D_{q}

Embedding dimension for key and query vectors in the transformer block

D_{v}

Embedding dimension for value vectors in the transformer block

D_{A}

Embedding dimension for the linear projection step in the transformer block

h

Number of attention heads in the transformer block

b

Batch size

D^{\prime}

Embedding dimension of the invariant layer’s output

Table 9:
Shapes of input terms used in the implementation

Term

Shape

[b,d,h,D,D_{v}]

[W]^{(O,i)}_{p,q}

[b,d,h,D_{v},D]

[WW]^{(QK,i)}_{p,q}

[b,d,h,D,D]

[b,d,D_{A},D]

I.1
Summary of Equivariant and Invariant Layers

We summarize our derived Equivariant and Invariant Layers from Appendix
D
and Appendix
E
. We use the bullet notation
\bullet
to simlify the notation, making it easier to implementation. Roughly speaking, the bullet stands for ”the parameter is unchanged when the bullet varies across all possible index values”.

I.1.1
Equivariant Layers with bullet notation

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(Q:i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(Q:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(Q:\bullet):p,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(Q:i)}_{p,q},

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(K:i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(K:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(K:\bullet):p,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(K:i)}_{p,q},

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(V:i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(V:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(V:\bullet):p,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(V:i)}_{p,q},

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(O:i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\left(\Phi^{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}_{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{1}\cdot\sum\limits_{k=1}^{D}[W]^{(O:i)}_{j,k}+\left(\Phi^{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}_{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{2}\cdot[W]^{(O:i)}_{j,k},

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(QK,\bullet):p,q}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(VO,\bullet):p,\bullet}\right)_{1}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(VO,\bullet):p,\bullet}\right)_{2}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,j}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{1}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{2}[W]^{(A)}_{j,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{3}[W]^{(A)}_{p,k}+\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{4}[W]^{(A)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(B):\bullet,q}\right)_{1}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(B):\bullet,q}\right)_{2}[W]^{(B)}_{k,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(A):\bullet}\right)_{1}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(A):\bullet}\right)_{2}[b]^{(A)}_{k}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet},

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(B)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k}_{(QK,\bullet):p,q}\left([WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k}_{(VO,\bullet):p,\bullet}\left([WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\left(\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k}_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{1}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k}_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{2}[W]^{(A)}_{p,j}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k}_{(B):\bullet,q}\right)_{1}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k}_{(B):\bullet,q}\right)_{2}[W]^{(B)}_{j,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\left(\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k}_{(A):\bullet}\right)_{1}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\left(\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k}_{(A):\bullet}\right)_{2}[b]^{(A)}_{j}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(B):\bullet,k},

\displaystyle[E(b)]^{(A)}_{k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\bullet}_{(QK,\bullet):p,q}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\bullet}_{(VO,\bullet):p,\bullet}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\left(\phi^{(A):\bullet}_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{1}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\left(\phi^{(A):\bullet}_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{2}[W]^{(A)}_{p,k}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet}_{(B):\bullet,q}\right)_{1}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet}_{(B):\bullet,q}\right)_{2}[W]^{(B)}_{k,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet}_{(A):\bullet}\right)_{1}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet}_{(A):\bullet}\right)_{2}[b]^{(A)}_{k}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\bullet}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(A):\bullet},

\displaystyle[E(b)]^{(B)}_{k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(QK,\bullet):p,q}\left([WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(VO,\bullet):p,\bullet}\left([WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):\bullet,q}[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(A):\bullet}[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(B):k}_{(B):q}[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi^{(B):k}.

I.1.2
Invariant Layers with bullet notation

\displaystyle I(U)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(QK,s):p,q}\cdot\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(VO,s):p,\bullet}\cdot\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi_{(A):\bullet,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(A)}_{p,q}+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D_{A}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(B):\bullet,q}\cdot[W]^{(B)}_{p,q}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D_{A}}\Phi_{(A):\bullet}\cdot[b]^{(A)}_{q}+\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi_{(B):q}\cdot[b]^{(B)}_{q}+\Phi_{1}

I.2
Equivariant Layers Pseudocode

I.2.1
[E(W)]^{(Q:i)}_{j,k}
Pseudocode

From the formula:

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(Q:i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(Q:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(Q:\bullet):p,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(Q:i)}_{p,q}

We define the pseudocode for each term:

\displaystyle\text{For}\ \Phi^{(Q:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(Q:\bullet):p,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(Q:i)}_{p,q},\text{ with }[W]^{(Q:i)}_{p,q}\ \text{of shape }[b,d,h,D,D_{q}]\ \text{and}\ \Phi^{(Q:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(Q:\bullet):p,\bullet}\ \text{of shape }[e,d,D,D]

\displaystyle\quad\text{Corresponding pseudocode:}\ \texttt{einsum}(bdhpk,edjp\rightarrow behjk)

I.2.2
[E(W)]^{(K:i)}_{j,k}
Pseudocode

From the formula:

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(K:i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(K:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(K:\bullet):p,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(K:i)}_{p,q}

We define the pseudocode for each term:

\displaystyle\text{For}\ \Phi^{(K:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(K:\bullet):p,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(K:i)}_{p,q},\text{ with }[W]^{(K:i)}_{p,q}\ \text{of shape }[b,d,h,D,D_{k}]\ \text{and}\ \Phi^{(K:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(K:\bullet):p,\bullet}\ \text{of shape }[e,d,D,D]

\displaystyle\quad\text{Corresponding pseudocode:}\ \texttt{einsum}(bdhpk,edjp\rightarrow behjk)

I.2.3
[E(W)]^{(V:i)}_{j,k}
Pseudocode

From the formula:

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(V:i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\Phi^{(V:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(V:\bullet):p,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(V:i)}_{p,q}

We define the pseudocode for each term:

\displaystyle\text{For}\ \Phi^{(V:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(V:\bullet):p,\bullet}\cdot[W]^{(V:i)}_{p,q},\text{ with }[W]^{(V:i)}_{p,q}\ \text{of shape }[b,d,h,D,D_{v}]\ \text{and}\ \Phi^{(V:\bullet):j,\bullet}_{(V:\bullet):p,\bullet}\ \text{of shape }[e,d,D,D]

\displaystyle\quad\text{Corresponding pseudocode:}\ \texttt{einsum}(bdhpk,edjp\rightarrow behjk)

I.2.4
[E(W)]^{(O:i)}_{j,k}
Pseudocode

From the formula:

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(O:i)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\left(\Phi^{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}_{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{1}\cdot\sum\limits_{k=1}^{D}[W]^{(O:i)}_{j,k}+\left(\Phi^{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}_{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{2}\cdot[W]^{(O:i)}_{j,k}

We define the pseudocode for each term:

\displaystyle\text{For}\left(\Phi^{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}_{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{1}\cdot\sum\limits_{k=1}^{D}[W]^{(O:i)}_{j,k},\text{ with }[W]^{(O:i)}_{j,k}\ \text{of shape }[b,d,h,D_{v},D]\ \text{and}\ \left(\Phi^{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}_{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{1}\ \text{of shape }[e,d]

\displaystyle\quad\text{Corresponding pseudocode:}\ \texttt{einsum}(bdhjk,ed\rightarrow behj)\ \texttt{.unsqueeze}(-1)

\displaystyle\text{For}\left(\Phi^{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}_{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{2}\cdot[W]^{(O:i)}_{j,k},\text{ with }[W]^{(O:i)}_{j,k}\ \text{of shape }[b,d,h,D_{v},D]\ \text{and}\ \left(\Phi^{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}_{(O:\bullet):\bullet,\bullet}\right)_{2}\ \text{of shape }[e,d]

\displaystyle\quad\text{Corresponding pseudocode:}\ \texttt{einsum}(bdhjk,ed\rightarrow behjk)

I.2.5
[E(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}
Pseudocode

From the formula:

\displaystyle[E(W)]^{(A)}_{j,k}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(QK,\bullet):p,q}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}[WW]^{{(QK,s)}}_{p,q}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{p=1}^{D}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{D}\left(\Phi^{(A):\bullet,\bullet}_{(VO,\bullet):p,\bullet}\right)_{1}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{h}[WW]^{(VO,s)}_{p,q}\right)
